# vSAN Host Internal API (field paths)

Generated by `tools/build.py` from [raw/live.json](raw/live.json); do not edit by hand.

`vim.host.VsanInternalSystem` on each ESX host (vim25, `HostSystem.configManager.vsanInternalSystem`) returns JSON strings rather than typed objects; this is an internal interface that Broadcom does not document. These are the JSON field paths seen on the live cluster. Instance-specific keys are normalized: `*` = UUID or device name, `#` = number (world ID, index, instance), `<controller>:<unit>` = virtual disk position. `QueryVsanStatistics` holds raw cumulative counters (bytes, counts, latency sums) from which the performance service derives its metrics.

Live evidence from `tools/live.py` (2026-10-19): vCenter and ESX 9.1.1, one OSA vSAN cluster with 3 hosts. Not enabled on that cluster, so their fields could not be seen: dedup, encryption, fileService, iscsi, stretchedOr2Node, perfVerboseMode.
